证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观点评

东吴证券研究所

2026年10月29日

《美国中期选举：决战前夕的四个关
键问题》 
2026-10-28 
《五个维度观察权益资产超跌信号》 
2026-10-26

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 
◼ 核心观点：10月FOMC会议以10:2维持政策利率不变，发布会上沃
什并未正面回应10月为何不加息，但结合其发言大致可归纳出不加息
的一些支撑依据：①9月已撤出部分货币宽松，其“药效”仍有待观察；
②当前指标不错，市场隐含通胀预期稳定；③下一阶段信息更丰富，呼
应声明中所述的需积累更多信息（等待中期选举的结果、中东局势的变
化、AI发展的走向） 。不过，由于沃什的这番发言“含糊其辞”，2-10年
美债利差一度走高10bps至33bps。向前看，美国核心CPI环比连续2
个月超市场一致预期，鉴于Higher for Longer的油价已经带来通胀扩
散风险，今年12月的剩余1次加息料为基准情形。而2027年的利率路
径仍将取决于中期选举结果对美国通胀和增长的影响。 
◼ FOMC声明：10:2维持利率不变。10月FOMC会议美联储以10-2投
票维持政策利率在[3.75, 4]%不变，符合市场与分析师的预期。其中美联
储最鹰派票委，克利夫兰联储主席Hammack和正被特朗普重启调查的
美联储理事Cook投票加息25bps。本次FOMC会议前市场对10月美
联储加息预期已不足10%，而发布会上沃什强调当前金融条件不够紧，
但对10月不加息的“含糊其辞”仍被市场解读为鸽派信号。导致美债
利率&美元指数走低，黄金走高，不过程度相对温和。截至最新，交易
员定价12月加息概率84%，明年3月累计加息1.8次，9月累计加息
2.8次。 
◼ 发布会：对10月不加息的决定“含糊其辞”。与9月比，10月FOMC
声明一方面将近期经济活动的描述从稳固（solid）上调至强劲（strong），
另一方面认为需要积累更多信息 帮助联储决策，以确保通胀的及时
（timely）回落。发布会上沃什则主要讨论了三个问题：议息会议改革、
回应10月不加息的决定、对利率影响AI的看法。 
◼ 具体来看：①联储改革方面，沃什表示，2027年将有1次不议息的“闭
门会议”，用以专注讨论货币政策战略，2028年将提升至2次。议息会
议次数的减少意味着政策决策更长的时滞，或对应资本市场更大的风险
溢价。②利率路径方面，沃什并未正面回应10月为何不加息，但结合
其发言中大致可归纳出不加息的一些支撑依据：9月已撤出部分货币宽
松其效果仍有待观察；当前经济指标表现良好，市场隐含通胀预期稳定；
下一阶段信息更丰富，呼应声明中所述的需积累更多信息，即等待中期
选举的结果、中东局势的变化、AI发展的走向。不过，由于沃什的这番
发言“含糊其辞”，2-10年美债利差一度走高10bps至33bps。③利率影
响方面，沃什描述了近期部分科技企业信用利差的走阔，表示投资者对
AI前景存在分歧。 
◼ 展望与策略：12月加息料为基准情形，2027年利率路径仍取决于中期
选举结果。我们认为，美联储10月加息的暂缓一方面受近期市场AI叙
事降温影响，另一方面受中选政治压力与衍生出的不确定性影响。向前
看，美国核心CPI环比连续2个月超市场一致预期（详见报告《美国9
月CPI：风险在油价的Higher for Longer——2026年9月美国CPI数据
点评》 ） ，鉴于Higher for Longer的油价已经带来通胀扩散风险，今年12
月的剩余1次加息料为基准情形。而2027年的利率路径取决于届时通
胀与增长的变化，而这两者均与中选结果息息相关：①国防支出结构变
化→中东&俄乌局势→油价→通胀；②美国财政的边际变化→增长；③
AI产业监管政策→增长（详见报告《美国中期选举观战指南》）。正如10
月 FOMC声明与发布会所言，当前美联储仍需积累更多信息，而下一
阶段信息将更加丰富。我们认为随着中期选举的尘埃落地，政策上的不
确定性逐步消解，当前相对混沌的市场交易可能才会重新“拨云见日”。 
◼ 风险提示：中东局势发展超预期；美联储维持高利率水平时间过长，引
发金融系统流动性危机；通胀下行速率不及预期。

投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6至 12个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。

### Page 1

Improving 3Q GPM helped by better mix — Dynapack's preliminary 3Q26 results 
showed margin recovery yet weak PC sales remained the key drag on the earnings. 
3Q26 EPS of NT$2.33 (+72% q/q and +49% y/y) missed our/BBG est by 45%/27%, 
although we believe the earnings shortfall was well anticipated given the 3Q sales 
miss owing to the delayed shipment of one of the BBU products and weaker PC 
demand. GPM improved q/q on a richer product mix, with higher-margin BBU 
making up c.50% of 3Q sales on our estimate, while OPM recovered to 10.7%, 
+4.2ppt q/q and +1.3ppt y/y. Nevertheless, the sales shortfall constrained op 
leverage, leaving margin improvement insufficient to offset weaker sales. 
PSU vendor shortages weigh on Oct; 12kW ramp to drive Nov sales rebound — 
Our industry checks suggest that Dynapack's major PSU vendor has encountered 
component supply shortages (related to power management IC) and therefore 
delayed BBU pull-ins, weighing on shipments of Dynapack's existing 3kW and 5kW 
BBU products in Oct. We therefore expect Oct-26 BBU sales to decline slightly m/m. 
Meanwhile, PC sales should recover sequentially and more than to offset the BBU 
softness, supporting a slight m/m increase in group sales. Looking beyond Oct-26, 
we expect accelerating shipments of the new 12kW BBU series from Nov-26 to drive 
a strong rebound in BBU sales.  
Buy 
Catalyst Watch: Upside, expires 26-NOV-26

Implications — We reiterate our bullish stance on the BBU sector despite near-term
volatility from external supply-chain constraints, which is likely to put pressure on
near-term sentiment. We view the component-driven delays in product pull-ins
primarily as a shipment-timing issue, with underlying demand remaining strong into
2027E. We also see ample opportunity for Dynapack to gain market share from

Panasonic, its major Japanese peer, particularly as power architectures migrate
toward HVDC. This share-gain potential remains intact, reinforcing our confidence
in Dynapack's medium term BBU growth outlook.

### Page 4

Dynapack International (3211.TWO)
19 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Dynapack International
Valuation
We assign a 32x target P/E to our 2027E EPS of NT$25 to derive a target price of NT$800. We value Dynapack based on 2027E
target P/E as we believe 2027E better reflects its earnings power after BBU capacity expansion. Our target P/E of 32x is based
on our forecast EPS CAGR of 66% in 2025-2027E and a PEG of 0.5x, which we benchmark against its major PSU partner, as we

expect Dynapack to grow alongside its partner.
Risks
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Dynapack. However, we believe such a rating is not warranted for
Dynapack due to: 1) the company is generating solid revenue and earnings growth over next 3 years; the underlying industry,
BBU for AIDC, also enjoys secular growth; 2) the business model that involves with product customization and strict
safety/reliability requirements would imply relatively benign competition and therefore stable pricing and margins; and 3) the

company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash position since 2019.
Key downside risks to our price target/rating are: 1) Slower-than-expected BBU adoption in AIDC, 2) Delay in PSU customer
qualifications, 3) Competition from larger battery or power solution vendors, 4) Safety, certification and reliability issues.
Panasonic Holdings
(6752.T; ¥3957.0; 1; 16 Oct 26; 15:30)
Valuation
To derive our target price, we use a global sum-of-the-parts (SoTP) approach. In the case of Panasonic Holdings, we reference

52 other companies in the Citi coverage universe to set a fair value PER for each business division, which we sum to derive our
overall target price.
Panasonic fair-value PERs for individual business segments range from 8x to 31x, and have a weighted average of 26.2x
(excluding subsidies in the battery business), taking minority interest and equity in earnings into account. We estimate
business-derived equity value of ¥5,000/share. We calculate the present value of the amount we expect Panasonic to receive

in battery subsidies over five years at ¥400/share. Combining these two values, we derive a target price of ¥5,400.
Risks
Risks to our target price include 1) unsuccessful management policies or strategies resulting in weaker-than-expected
business investment returns or sales growth, or conversely producing greater-than-expected results at a relatively early stage,
2) stagnation in the global economy or an upturn in it, and rises or declines in demand in the electrical industry, and 3) forex

trends. If these factors manifest themselves differently than we have anticipated, the share price may vary from our target
price.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

M
Idea
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
3Q preview – margin upside;
MLCC tightening thesis intact
3Q revenue exceeded guidance, and we expect OpM of 29.1%,
140bps above the Street. Robust AI and general server demand,
together with pricing, should offset consumer softness,
supporting 4Q revenue and margin guidance of “up slightly q/q.”
Stay OW; price target unchanged.
3Q preview –  better than guidance, driven by stronger volumes and pricing:
Unaudited revenue reached ~NT$49.5bn (+11% q/q, +50% y/y), stronger than

management's guidance of a "moderate increase q/q" (which we interpret as mid- to
high-single-digit growth). We did highlight in our results takeaway report (link) that
there was no pushback from management when we asked whether the guidance
was on the conservative side. We think  some conservatism was baked into the
guidance in view of uncertainties and limited visibility into the consumer segment.
As a result of the stronger top line, we now expect GM to surpass 40%, reaching

40.4% (+190bps q/q, +420bps y/y), and for OpM to increase to 29.1% (+270bps q/q,
+620bps y/y). This is still considered in line with management's  margin guidance of
up "slightly" q/q (which we interpret as 0-3% sequential growth). With the slightly
stronger top line and margin forecast, we now estimate 3Q net profit to come in at
~NT$11.6bn (+23% q/q, +82% y/y) for EPS of NT$5.63.
4Q outlook –  pricing and AI demand should continue to drive revenue growth

and margin expansion: Consumer demand is likely going to weaken in 4Q, based on
our supply chain checks, as well as PC demand coming in weaker than expected in
3Q, per IDC. However, AI and general server demand should remain robust, and
Yageo should continue to see strong momentum for non-consumer related passive
component demand.  As a result, we see management issuing 4Q revenue guidance
of "up slightly q/q" and GM/OpM guidance of "up slightly q/q".

Key issues on which we will focus in the earnings call: 1) demand visibility for non-
AI; 2) pricing actions for MLCC in 1Q, 3) pricing actions for tantalum, resistors,
magnetics, etc., 4) customer inventory levels for consumer, 5) thoughts on the TDK/
Taiyo Yuden MOU, 6) view on midrange/low-end capacity shortage in CY27.
Reiterate OW with price target at NT$1,050: This implies ~24x CY28e P/E;
however, PEG is only 0.72x. We favor Yageo for its scale, broad product portfolio

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Price target discussion and valuation methodology
Our 12-month price target is unchanged at NT$1,050:  Our price target is our base case
value, derived from our residual income valuation model. Similar to our analysis of other
tech hardware companies within our coverage, we think this methodology provides the
most accurate value of the firm by taking into account the cost of equity.
Our key assumptions are all unchanged, including:

• Cost of equity of 7.8%
° Risk-free rate of 1.0% (10-year Taiwanese government bond yield)
° Equity risk premium of 6.8%
° Beta of 1.1
• Medium-term growth rate of 12%
• Terminal growth rate of 3%
Our price target implies 32x 2027e P/E and 24x 2028e P/E. These are above its five-year
historical range of 8x to 18x, which we view as justified considering its longer-term
transformation story to become a global total solutions provider, moving from passive

into active components. In addition, compared to global passive component peers, we
believe Yageo stock remains undervalued with higher ROE and margins but lower P/E vs.
Japanese peers (which trade on an average at 30x+ P/E).
Our bull and bear case scenario values are also unchanged at NT$1,490 and NT$480,
respectively.

Risk Reward - Yageo Corp. (2327.TW)
NT$1,050.00
Base case, multistage residual income valuation model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 7.8%
(risk-free rate 1%, equity risk premium 6.8% and beta of 1.1), medium-term growth rate 12%,
terminal growth rate 3%.
Morgan Stanley EstimatesMean
Consensus Price Target Distribution NT$740.00 NT$1,450.00
Source: Reﬁnitiv, Morgan Stanley Research
MS Rating
▪ There is fear that leading Japanese and

Korean MLCC suppliers are shifting more
capacity to cater to the high end, resulting in
undersupply for the midrange/low end.
▪ The same narrative that drove the 2018
cycle is now back in play.
▪ Utilization rates are increasing, so
suppliers are expecting price hikes for the
midrange/low end. Distributors and ODMs
are both building inventory.
▪ AI demand is growing and tantalum
capacitor pricing may show further price
increases in 2H.
▪ All of this should help support a re-rating

in the stock.

NT$1,490.00
34x 2028e EPS
Demand continues to grow faster than
expected in 2027-28: 1) Demand improves
signiﬁcantly, driving pricing power for Yageo
in premium and standard products alike. 2)

Yageo makes meaningful progress into AI,
driving signiﬁcant share gains. 3) Yageo
takes on more acquisition initiatives to
further widen its product portfolio. 4)
Severe undersupply leads to continued price
hikes for an extended period of time, which
helps expand Yageo's margins in 2027-28.
NT$1,050.00
24x 2028e EPS
Demand remains strong into 2027-28: 1)
Utilization remains high for premium
products. 2) Utilization for standard
products stabilizes at 70%+ with resilient

consumer tech demand. 3) Transformation
and synergies are ongoing with continued
active initiatives. 4) Yageo continues to drive
expansion into the AI realm and slowly gains
market share. 5) High utilization rates and
inﬂationary raw material costs result in
continued price hikes.
NT$480.00
11x 2028e EPS
Earnings erode as macro conditions weaken
and demand falls again in 2027-28: 1) Non-AI
demand weakens from 2H26 onward,
despite AI demand remaining strong. 2)

Pricing power shifts to end customers with
heightened competition among peers. 3)
Acquisitions do not bear fruit, resulting in
layoffs and leading to cost burdens. 4)
Automotive and industrial end demand does
not recover, hampering Yageo's premium
business. 5) Yageo does not make any
material advancements into AI.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

以上資料為康和投顧所提供,不得轉寄                                                                         
本公司不負任何法律責任，請投資人自行斟酌參考

信邦(3023 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子中游 
投 資 速 報 
買進-首次 目標價 NT$373 (潛在報酬+35.6%) 2026-10-28

半導體設備連接線需求 強勁  
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
基 於 以 下 理 由 ， 研 究 部 給 予 信 邦 逢 低 買 進 評 等  
1. 1~3 Q26 營 收 達 271.83 億 元 ， 年 增 15.24% ， 3~9 月 營 收 連 增 7 個月 ，
表 現 優 於 市 場 預 期 。  
2. 工 業 （ 半 導 體 設 備 、 自 動 化 ） 、 醫 療 及 車 用 連 接 線 為 三 大 長 期 成 長 引 擎 。  
3. 公 司 半 導 體 設 備 從 線 束 升 級 至 機 櫃 組 裝 大 幅 提 升 附 加 價 值 與 能 見 度；汽 車
業 務 受 惠 電 動 化 與 控 制 模 組 放 量；醫 療 則 受 惠 中 國 市 場 回 溫 可 提 供 近 兩
年 明 確 動 能，人 形 機 器 人 則 為 2028 年 後 的 爆 發 潛 力， 預估 2027 年 EPS  
17.78 元 PE 約 15. 5X， 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標 價 為 373 元 (21* 202 7 年 E P S )。

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介  
公司是台灣領先的電子零件整合設計與製造服務商，專注於客製化線束
（ Cable Assembly ） 、 精 密 連 接 器 組 件 及 系 統 整 合 解 決 方 案 。 核 心 業 務 以 提
供從設計、打樣、製造到組裝的完整解決方案。產品以連接線組為主（約
78% ） ， 連 接 器 及 零 組 件 約22% 。 代 理 Hirose 等 國 際 大 廠 連 接 器 。 客 戶 涵 蓋
全球前十大風機廠商中的 8 家、前 十 大 醫 療 成 像 設 備 商 中 的 6 家、前 二 十 大
工業設備商中的 9 家、前十大汽車商中的 6 家等。 2025 年產品應用占比大
致為工業約 31% 、通訊 24% 、 綠 能 20% 、 汽 車 / 航太 16% 、 醫 療 9% （實際
會 隨 年 度 波 動 ） 。 銷 售 地 區 以 中 國 大 陸 為 主（ 約53% ） ， 其 次 為 美 國 、 台 灣 、
歐洲等。積極擴充半導體設備機櫃組裝產能及商用無人機相關產品。  
圖 一 、 1~3Q26 產 品 應 用 佔 比

2

全球半導體設備市場規模快速成長  
根 據 市 調 機 構 數 據 顯 示 ， 全 球 前 段 製 程 設 備 （ Waf er Fa b Equipmen t, 
WFE ）， 2025 年 銷 售 額 創 下 1,169 億 美 元 新 高 ， 預 估 2026 年將再成長
23.1% ，達 1,439 億 美 元 。 此 數 字 較 SEMI 先 前 於 2025 年 底 所 公 布 的 預 測
大 幅 上 修 ， 主 要 受 惠 於 先 進 記 憶 體 、 尤 其 是 HBM 相關 DRAM 技 術 ， 以 及
先 進 邏 輯 應 用 投 資 持 續 增 加 。 隨 著 製 造 商 因 應 AI 應 用 需 求 ， 加 速 擴 充 產 能
及 推 進 技 術 升 級，預 估 2027 年及 2028 年 將 分 別 成 長 21.8% 及 14.1% ，2028
年 市 場 規 模 可 望 突 破 2,000 億 美 元 。  
圖 二 、 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 市 場 預 測

資 料 來 源 ： SEMI 、 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

工業應用為 最 強 成 長 動 能  
1~3Q26 營 收 占 比 37% ， YOY+38.9% ， 主 要 受 惠 於 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 需 求 擴
張 及 智 能 製 造 自 動 化 升 級 潮 。 內 部 結 構 佔 比 包括 倉 儲 物 流 自 動 化 近 40% 、
半 導 體 設 備 佔 30 ~35% 、 運 輸 / 建設 設 備 線 束 佔 10% 、 能 源 管 理 類 佔 1 0% 。
動能 主 要 來 自 半 導 體 設 備 機 內 / 機 外 訊 號 與 電 源 線 束 組 裝 以 及 倉 儲 物 流 自
動 化 需 求 增 加 ， 27F 營收 YOY+20% 。  
a. 半 導 體 設 備 : 為 最 大 亮 點 ， 從 傳 統 線 束 升 級 至 整 機 機 櫃 組 裝 與 系 統 整 合 。
提 供 機 內 ／ 機 外 訊 號 線 與 電 源 線，並 開 始 整 合 其 他 零 組 件 做 成 小 型 機 櫃
／ 模 組 給 客 戶 。  
✓ 供 應 鏈 地 位 ： 公司 為 全 球 半 導 體 前 段 與 後 段 設 備 大 廠 （ 如  AS ML 、應
用 材 料 等 ） 的 重 要 高 度 客 製 化 線 束 及 系 統 整 合 供 應 商 。  
✓ 應 用 領 域 ： 先 進 節 點 製 造 中 的 光 刻 （ 微 影 ） 、 蝕 刻 和 沉 積 設 備 、 精密
製 程 設 備 中 的 線 束 系 統 、 機 櫃 半 成 品 組 裝 。  
✓ 訂 單 與 產 能 狀 況： 受 產 品 銷 量 增 長 、 複 雜 性 提 升 及 新 客 戶 訂 單 需 求 驅
動，公 司 目 前 通 過 加 班 和 增 加 第 二 班 次 滿 足 產 能 需 求 ；為 進 一 步 擴
充 產 能 ， 斥 資 逾 50 億 元 在 苗 栗 銅 鑼 科 學 園 區 廠 房 擴 建 產 能 ， 主 要
目 的 即 是 用 於 擴 充 半 導 體 設 備 高 階 模 組 與 線 束 生 產 量 能 。 計 劃 於
2Q27 年 正 式 投 入 運 營 ， 預計 增 長 趨 勢 將 持 續 到 2030 年 。

✓ 液 冷 散 熱 技 術 延 伸 : 已 展 示 四 款 數 據 中 心 熱 管 理 解 決 方 案 原 型（ 兩 款 浸

理 服 務 ， 產品預計 2026 年 下 半 年 開 始 送 樣 ， 2H27 年 對 盈 利 產 生
實 質 性 貢 獻 。  
b. 人 形 機 器 人 ： 機 器 人 線 束 預 估 4Q26 起進 入 量 產 ， 已 取 得 5 家美系 Tier 1
獨 家 訂 單 ， 提 供 整 組 線 束 模 組 （ 獨 家 供 應 ） ， 終 端 應 用 多 在 工 廠 或 倉 儲
物 流 。 預 估 營 收 占 比 2026 年約 1% 、 2027 年約 2% 、 2028 年 有 望 達
13% 。 毛 利 率 預 估 約 40% ， 2028 年 有 機 會 達 10 萬 套 以 上 。  
✓ 產品 : 提 供 完 整 線 纜 模 組 （ 感 測 器 、 連 接 器 、 訊 號 傳 輸 線 、 充 電 線 、 關
節 線 束 等 ） ，涵 蓋 全 身 應 用，單 台 機 器 人 線 束 產 值 約 1,000 – 1,500
美 元 （ 視 整 合 程 度 ， 一 台 可 能 需 要 50 – 150 條 線 束 ） 。  
✓ 技 術 門 檻 高：關 節 線 束 需 通 過 極 高 次 數（ 數 十 萬 至 百 萬 次 ）彎 折 測 試 ，
具 備 耐 彎、耐 壓、防 水、輕 量 化 等 特 性，屬 客 製 化 總 解 決 方 案 （Tota l 
Solution ） ， 多 數 專 案 為 獨 家 供 應 （ Only S our ce ） 。  
✓ 客 戶 ： 已 取 得 約 5 家美系 Tier1 （ 包 括 Figure 等 ） 獨 家 線 束 訂 單 ， 部
分 涵 蓋 中 國 客 戶 （ 以 零 組 件 為 主 ） 。 深 耕 機 器 人 領 域 逾 10 年，從
AGV 、 機 械 手 臂 、 機 器 狗 累 積 經 驗 。  
✓ 策 略 聯 盟 ： 與 宇 隆（ 2233 ）合 作 ， 結 合 線 束 / 控 制 模 組 與 減 速 機（ RV 、
諧 波 、 行 星 ） ， 朝 系 統 級 解 決 方 案 發 展 。  
表 一 、 其他 機 器 人 類 型

資 料 來 源 ： 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

c. 無 人 機 : 主要 提 供 無 人 機 機 內 線 及 電 池 換 電 櫃 櫃 體 ， 主 要 供 應 2 家 美 系 客
戶。 3Q26 起 開 始 出 貨 ， 26 年 營 收 約 5~6 億元 ， 27 年 估 成 長 至 8~10
億 元 ， 毛 利 率 約 有 30~40% ， 預 計 於 2028 年 放 量 。  
✓ 定位 : 採 取 差 異 化 策 略 ， 聚 焦 高 法 規 門 檻 與 營 運 壁 壘 的 「 商 用 運 輸 」 市
場 （ 物 流 配 送 等 ） ， 避 開 競 爭 激 烈 的 消 費 型 空 拍 應 用 。  
✓ 產品 : 機 內 線 束（ 充 電 線 、 訊 號 傳 輸 線 等 ）、 換 電 櫃（ 電 池 交 換 櫃 體 ），
支 援 快 速 電 池 更 換 。  
✓ 客 戶 ： 以 美 系 為 主 ， 包 括 Ama zon 等 物 流 無 人 機 相 關 業 者 （ 提 供 整 組
解 決 方 案 ） ， 目 前 約 服 務 2 家 美 國 主 要 客 戶 。  
✓ 應用 : 倉 儲 到 配 送 站 點 的 物 流 運 輸 、 最 後 一 哩 路 精 準 配 送 、 與 倉 儲 機 器

人 、 智 慧 安 全 背 心 形 成 完 整 智 慧 物 流 生 態 系 。  
表 二 、 智 慧 倉 儲 無 人 化 生 態 系 統

資 料 來 源 ： 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

醫 療 及 車 用 產 品 穩 定 成 長  
醫療 ： 1~3Q26 營 收 佔 比 9.8% ， YOY+28.4% ， 以 病 人 監 護 與 影 像 診 斷 設 備
內 部 之 傳 輸 線 束 為 主 ( 中系 ) ， GPM> 平 均 ， 27F 營收 YOY+10~15% 。  
a. 產品 : 專 注 於 高 階 大 型 醫 療 設 備 線 束 ， 包 括  CT （ 電 腦 斷 層 ） 、 MRI （ 核 磁
共 振 ） 、 超 音 波 診 斷 設 備  及  加 護 病 房 （ ICU ） 病 人 監 護 系 統 。  
b. 技 術 壁 壘 ： 醫 療 線 束 要 求 極 高 的 生 物 相 容 性 、 抗 干 擾 性 （ EMI ） 與 長 期 高
穩 定 度 ， 認 證 時 間 長 達 2~3 年 以 上 ， 屬 於 「 高 客 製 化 、 高 利 基 」 市 場 。 
c. 成 長 動 能 : 受 惠 中 國 客 戶 加 大 出 口 與 海 外 專 案 進 入 量 產 ， 2026 年 成 長 明
顯 ， 毛 利 率 優 於 平 均 ， 2027 年 預 估 成 長 10 – 15% ， 提 供 穩 定 貢 獻 與 產
品 組 合 優 化 效 果 。  
d. 客戶 : 包括 GE Health car e 、Ph ilips 及 S iemens Healthin eers ， 產 品 滲 透
率 持 續 提 升 。  
車用 :1~3Q26 營 收 占 比 16.5% ， YOY+25.5% ， 充 電 線 （ 電 動 車 與 充 電 樁 ）
及 一 般 汽 車 電 子 訊 號 線（ 胎 壓 偵 測 、 360 度 環 景 、 倒 車 雷 達 等 ） 表 現 持 穩 ，
主 要 成 長 動能 來 自 控 制 器 ， 整 合 5 個 E CU 功 能 （ 含 引 擎 控 制 、 熱 管 理 、 電
池管理、訊號控 制等），提升運算效率。主要客戶為上汽， 27F 營收
YOY+10~15% 。  
a. 產品 : 包 括 充 電 線（ 電 動 車 與 充 電 樁 ）； 一 般 汽 車 電 子 訊 號 線（ 胎 壓 偵 測 、
360 度 環 景 、 倒 車 雷 達 等 ） ； PDCU （ Power Domain Con tr ol Un it ） 控 制
器： PCB 組 裝 產 品 ， 將 過 去 約 5 個 ECU 功 能 （ 引 擎 控 制 、 熱 管 理 、 電 池 管
理 、 訊 號 控 制 等 ） 整 合 成 一 顆 「 大 控 制 器 」 ， 為 26~27 年 主 要 成 長 來 源 。  
b. 主要出貨給中國油電混合車客戶（以上海汽車體系為主，部分品牌有出
口 ） ， 並 擴 展 至 日 韓 等 市 場 。  
c. 展望 : 純 電 動 車 充 電 相 關 今 年 大 致 持 平，成 長 主 要 來 自 PD CU 控 制 器。2027
年 預 估 成 長 10 – 15% 。 長 期 受 惠 電 動 化 與 高 電 壓 ／ 大 電 流 解 決 方 案 需 求 。

研 究  報 告                
力積電(6770.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
記憶體漲價貢獻 4Q26 獲利維持高檔，規劃 3D AI Foundry 新廠  
 投資評價與建議 
3Q26獲利優於預期，且4Q26毛利率有望重回50%以上之高峰，加上明年記憶
體、成熟製程市況展望正向，故我們給予2.3倍PB，持續看好後續營運動能。 
 重點評論分析 
看好4Q26毛利率重回過往50%以上高峰： 基於 ： (1)IPC 、 網通和醫療等應用支
撐力積電DRAM代工需求，Flash業務中，SLC NAND則因記憶體大廠先進製
程產能排擠，獲得車用、網通等小容量應用降規需求挹注；(2) AI伺服器持續
支撐PMIC、分離式元件需求，另考量中介層與Wafer on Wafer於新竹廠區陸
續復線，將有利於3D AI Foundry營收動能回溫。預估有望季增18.3%至252.4
億元，毛利率53.6%，稅後EPS 1.86元。  
正向看待明年展望： 展望明年 ， 我們認為 ： (1)AI需求維持強勁且應用持續擴大 ，
預期記憶體供不應求之情況將至少延續至 2027年，其中市場預估明年DRAM
報價將延續漲勢；(2)邏輯業務中，PMIC、分離式元件等8吋產能持續受惠AI強
勁需求，我們預期明年台系成熟製程廠商產能利用率有望達90%以上，公司規
劃明年漲價約10~20%且產能將以LTA客戶為優先；(3)考量目前無塵室空間幾
乎用罄，公司將開始評估新廠並著重3D AI Foundry產能擴產，但計畫透過設
備租賃、客戶給付預付款形式由客戶參與部分投資金額，以此控制公司資本支
出。我們初步預估明年營收達1,241.8億元，年增60.3%，稅後EPS 10.65元。 
 投資風險 
記憶體報價下滑、3D AI Foundry進展不如預期。

| 營運概況  
3Q26獲利優於預期 ：力積電公告3Q26自結營收213.4億元 ， 季增23.4% 、 年增80.3% ， 毛利率47.6% ， 稅後EPS 1.38
元，獲利表現優於預期，因毛利率受惠於平均ASP季增24%、產能利用率由2Q26的87%提升至3Q26的97%。 
4Q26營收雙位數成⻑，看好毛利率重回過往50%以上高峰：展望4Q26，基於：(1)記憶體大廠優先投資HBM並逐
步退出利基型DRAM市場，加上IPC、網通和醫療等應用支撐，使得力積電DRAM代工需求維持穩健，至於與美光
合作之1P製程進展順利，預計2Q27開始試產，Flash業務中，SLC NAND則因記憶體大廠先進製程產能排擠，獲得
車用、網通等小容量應用降規需求挹注，另公司維持MLC NAND今年底tape out之進度；(2)邏輯業務中，儘管消
費性電子需求不佳，但AI伺服器需求強勁持續支撐PMIC、分離式元件需求，其中公司PMIC代工服務已從0.15微米
切入55奈米並開始出貨，且獲客戶三年綁約，反映需求強勁，另考量中介層與Wafer on Wafer於新竹廠區陸續復
線，將有利於4Q26 AI Foundry營收動能回溫。綜上所述，公司估本季營收有望雙位數季增，我們則預估有望季增
18.3%至252.4億元，毛利率53.6%，回到過往50%以上之高峰水準，稅後EPS 1.86元。  
正向看待明年展望：展望明年，我們認為：(1)AI需求維持強勁且應用持續擴大，預期記憶體大廠優先投資先進製程
之策略不變，故認為整體記憶體市場供不應求之情況將至少延續至2027年，其中市場預估明年DRAM報價將延續
漲勢(但漲幅收斂)，隱含公司代工價格具上漲動能；(2)邏輯業務中，半導體庫存維持健康，且PMIC、分離式元件等
8吋產能持續受惠AI強勁需求(其中分離式元件80%產能已簽訂LTA) ， 我們預期明年台系成熟製程廠商產能利用率有
望達90%以上，基於產能吃緊將有利於明年成熟製程廠商議價能力，公司規劃明年漲價約10~20%且產能將以LTA
客戶為優先；(3)3D AI Foundry業務仍為未來投資主軸，且產能規劃與進展大致如期，唯⼀有較新進展之業務則為
Wafer on Wafer規劃1H27量產 ， 另公司考量目前無塵室空間幾乎用罄 ， 公司將開始評估新廠並著重3D AI Foundry
產能擴產，但計畫透過設備租賃、客戶給付預付款形式由客戶參與30~60%的投資金額，以此控制公司資本支出。
我們初步預估明年營收達1,241.8億元，年增60.3%，稅後EPS 10.65元。

程曦資訊深耕客服委外市場 超過 30 年，具備長期公部門合作關
係、高客戶續約率及跨產業客服營運經驗，建立穩定營收基礎與市
場競爭優勢。隨 AI 技術逐步導入既有客服流程，有望降低人力成
本並提升營運效率，加上 AI 客服平台及軟體訂閱服務持續發展，
預期將帶動產品組合與 獲利結構改善。此外，公司取得 ISO/IEC 
42001 人工智慧管理系統認證，有助於提升高規格企業客戶的信
任度，進一步拓展民營企業市場。然而考量 AI 新事業仍處於發展
初期，短期研發費用投入較高，實際獲利貢獻仍需時間顯現，綜合
以上，研究部預估 程曦資訊 2027 年 營 收 為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%， 毛利率25.55%，Y+1.15ppts， 營業利益率15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts， 稅後純益1.93 億元 ，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元，
參考同業本益比區間約 10~18 倍，給予 15 倍本益比評價，目標
價為 88 元，投資建議為買進。 
 程曦資訊(6898.TT)公司簡介 
程曦資訊(6898.TT)成立於 1993 年 8 月，於 2022 年 5 月登錄興櫃
戰略新板，同年 9 月轉至興櫃一般板，並於 2026 年 10 月 23 日正
式掛牌上櫃交易 。 公司為台灣專業企業流程委外(BPO)及客服營運服
務商，深耕客服市場逾 30 年，以「系統、流程、人力」一站式整合
服務為核心，結合 AI 技術與營運管理經驗，提供企業及政府機關完
整的客服委外解決方案。客戶結構涵蓋政府機關、國營事業、金融
保險、電子商務及國際品牌等，具備大型政府專案執行經驗與長期
客戶合作基礎。營運布 局方面，公司於台北、桃園、台中、台南、
高雄及屏東設有據點，並透過子公司程高資訊、點鑽整合行銷及印
尼程曦，分別拓展中南部客服市場、會員數據行銷及東南亞多語系
客服業務，持續擴大服務範疇與市場規模。

傾聽顧客需求並優化服務流程。

售經驗 ， 協助客戶將客服中心由成本中心轉型為利潤中心 。 
1.4 全通路服務 ： 整合電話 、Webchat、LINE、FB Messenger、
IG 等多軌管道，提供文字談話、論壇管理與社群服務。

諮詢 、 線上故障排除與報修支援 （如OPPO、Realme、Plaud 
AI 等客戶） 。

營運現況 
公部門與企業客服專案帶動成長，研發及人事費用增加壓抑獲利 
程曦資訊 1H26 營收為 6.22 億元，Y+11.28%，主要受惠公部門及
企業客戶客服委外需求持續成長，帶動常態性年約服務與任務型專
案收入增加。其中，常態性年約服務涵蓋移工服務、社會住宅及長
照等政府專案，提供穩定營收基礎；任務型專案則受惠普發現金客
服專案認列，加上企業 AI 客服及數位轉型需求增加，推升整體營運
規模。獲利方面，受惠高毛利任務型專案貢獻增加，以及人力調度

服務穩定貢獻，加上國合會熊本賑災 1988 專線、世界盃足球賽期
間台灣運彩客服擴編等任務型專案挹注。展望4Q26，隨運動部 「揮
汗有禮」活動持續推進，加上政府及企業年度客服委外需求延續，
預期營收可望維持成長。獲利方面，公司持續 將 AI 智能質檢、AI 
Trainer 及 AI Agent 等工具導入客服流程，雖有助於降低人力工時
及提升服務效率，但短期研發與人事費用增加，預期仍將壓抑營業
利益率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊2026 年營收為 12.80

AI 客服座艙提升營運效率，軟體訂閱服務推動獲利結構改善 
展望 2027 年，程曦資訊將持續推動 AI 客服座艙與全通路服務平台
整合，透過 AI IVR 、即時知識查詢、自動化通話摘要及智能質檢等
功能，降低客服人員重複性作業與教育訓練成本。隨相關應用逐步
導入既有客服專案，預期可降低營收成長對人力擴編的依賴，改善
傳統 BPO 業務的人力成本結構；另一方面，公司規劃將客服系統與
AI 應用模組轉化為雲端租賃服務，透過月費訂閱及功能加購增加軟
體收入來源， 公司目標 2027 年 AI 相關新業務營收占比 可望 達
5%~8%。考量初期收入規模仍有限，預期2027 年獲利改善主要來
自既有業務效率提升，隨後續訂閱客戶增加，長期有望進一步帶動
毛利率成長。

AI 治理認證強化競爭優勢，民營企業市場拓展擴大成長空間 
程曦資訊目前營收主要來自公部門， 2025 年公部門營收占比達
76%，未來將在既有公部門業務基礎上，積極拓展金融、能源、交
通及電商等民營企業市場，擴大跨產業客戶服務規模。10M26 公司
取得 ISO/IEC 420 01 人工智慧管理系統認證，結合既有 ISO/IEC 
27001 資訊安全管理能力，可望強化AI 應用在資料保護、風險管理
及法規遵循方面的可信賴度，提高承接大型企業 客服專案的競爭
力。另一方面，公司可透過既有客服營運經驗，進一步提供AI 流程
改造 、 數據分析及顧問服務 ， 增加單一客戶的服務項目與收入貢獻。
綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊 2027 年營收為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%，毛利率 25.55%，Y+1.15ppts，營業利益率 15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts，稅後純益 1.93 億元，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元。 
圖三、程曦資訊 2025 年客戶組成

資料來源：公司資料、第一金投顧彙整

結論與建議 
程曦資訊深耕客服委外市場超過 30 年，具備長期公部門合作關係、
高客戶續約率及跨產業客服營運經驗，建立穩定營收基礎與市場競
爭優勢。隨 AI 技術逐步導入既有客服流程，有望降低人力成本並提
升營運效率，加上 AI 客服平台及軟體訂閱服務持續發展，預期將帶
動產品組合與獲利結構改善。此外，公司取得 ISO/IEC 42001 人工
智慧管理系統認證，有助於提升高規格企業客戶的信任度，進一步

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports. 1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.
Exhibit 1: FDI Inflows to the U.S.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026, 2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.
A) Higher 10-year Yield Consistent with Strong Nominal Growth Environment. B) Domestic Private Investors Now Dominate U.S. Treasury Market.

Investment Implications
In our view, higher nominal and real
yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

### Page 2

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

2027 is likely to be another eventful year for European utilities.
Geopolitical uncertainty, volatility in energy markets and
signiﬁcant investment pipelines, together with the associated
funding needs, should remain key drivers for the sector going into
next year.
We forecast €75bn of total bond supply from investment-grade
utilities in 2027, only slightly below the €80bn we have pencilled in
for full-year 2026. Net supply also remains ﬁrmly positive at €35bn

next year, although somewhat below the €44bn in 2026.

High capital expenditure drives supply
Alongside redemptions, bond supply continues to be driven by
large capital expenditure. We expect combined investment to top
€200bn next year for large European utility issuers. German names

have the highest investment needs for 2027, up around 30% year-
on-year, while Italian, UK, and Dutch names are also among those
seeing a clear increase.
The energy transition continues to drive signiﬁcant investment
needs, with growing electriﬁcation and renewable generation
requiring substantial expansion and reinforcement of transmission
and distribution grids. Regulated utilities are particularly impacted
by higher grid investments, but integrated utilities are also likely to

see higher capital expenditure next year.

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Sticking with the plan
• The RBNZ left the OCR at 2.75% - a shock to
the market.
• The committee voted 4:3 against a 25bp
hike, with Governor Breman within the
“on hold” camp.
• The RBNZ delivered on its September strategy
of pausing to assess the impact of OCR
increases done to date.
• The MPC remains of the view that the OCR will
increase eventually, although views on the
timing differ among members.
• Westpac expects a 25bp hike in December and

continues to expect an eventual peak OCR of
4% in September next year.
• However, a December hike is not a done
deal and will depend on data on fuel prices,
inflation expectations and second-round
pricing pressures and information on evolving
global economic risks that accumulate in the
next six weeks.
Official Cash Rate forecasts

RBNZ September 2026 MPS
